## Method and external sources

Every production row was joined to all Tracks, Releases, normalized ISRCs, platform links, credits, legacy metadata, and its current MusicBrainz Recording entry. Version identity uses explicit release/track/disambiguation evidence. Matching duration or source identifier alone never produces a confirmed merge; weak cases say `AUDIO REVIEW REQUIRED`.

Sources consulted: [official Juan Luis Guerra history](https://juanluisguerra.com/historia/), [official site/discography](https://juanluisguerra.com/), [MusicBrainz group catalog](https://musicbrainz.org/artist/1659a41f-27d3-4823-8a15-bf7cd295cf59), [MusicBrainz solo catalog](https://musicbrainz.org/artist/c3dcad1b-1380-4a94-98d8-3f8272f19df6), [Apple Music artist catalog](https://music.apple.com/es/artist/juan-luis-guerra-4-40/1474382045), [Spotify artist catalog](https://open.spotify.com/artist/3nlpTZci9O5W8RsNoNH559), and official YouTube/live uploads where linked below. Discogs and platform URLs are treated as corroborating evidence, not authority.
